// ================================================================================
// ARCANIZER SHARDS - UNIVERSAL ENCHANTMENT SYSTEM
// ================================================================================
//
// Two subsystems:
// 1. WEAPON SHARDS: Direct boosts on weapon (melee priority, ranged fallback)
// 2. EQUIPMENT SHARDS: Boosts on character via Status Transfer system
//
// Pricing & loot frequency (audited 2026-05-15 against real distribution):
//   Uncommon  → Rarity "Uncommon",  ValueOverride 50   (Shop 400),   pool freq 48
//   Rare      → Rarity "Rare",      ValueOverride 100  (Shop 1000),  pool freq 24
//   VeryRare  → Rarity "VeryRare",  ValueOverride 300  (Shop 3000),  pool freq 16
//   Legendary → Rarity "Legendary", ValueOverride 1000 (Shop 7000),  pool freq 1
//   Pool = AMP_Shard_DamageType_Pool (main loot pool, 270 entries)
//   Shop variants (`_Shop` suffix) use `using` non-Shop parent + override ValueOverride.
//
// ================================================================================

// ================ SHARD OBJECTS ================
